Abstract: Existing autonomous-driving world models typically perform dense prediction of future videos, occupancy states, BEV representations, or agent motion. We argue that planning need not reconstruct the complete future world, but only focus on scene features that affect future ego action. Based on this perspective, we propose Auto-JEPA, an action-oriented latent world model that learns continuous future driving intent through joint-embedding prediction. Given visual observations, egomotion history, and navigation commands, Auto-JEPA predicts an intent embedding aligned with the latent representation of the future ego trajectory. The predicted intent retrieves executable trajectories from a fixed trajectory memory, which are then ranked by a scene-conditioned candidate selection module. Auto-JEPA keeps the visual encoder frozen, requires no explicit perception annotations, and uses no learned trajectory generator. By optimizing only task-specific modules for trajectory representation, intent prediction, and candidate selection, Auto-JEPA achieves 91.3 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 89.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2. Semantic occlusion experiments show that masking dynamic-agent regions induces an average intent change 2.97x that of equal-area random masking. Moreover, occluding vehicles that affect future driving substantially changes the predicted intent and selected trajectory, whereas both remain essentially unchanged when non-influential vehicles are occluded. These results show that future-intent prediction encourages the model to focus on planning-relevant visual features and supports high-quality planning without dense future-world modeling.

</details>


### [82] [Outcome-Guided Distillation: A Teacher-Student Framework to Advance VLM Reasoning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29052)
*Zeyu Dong,Yimin Zhu,Yu Wu,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于教师-学生架构的新框架，通过整合结构化推理和几何精度来提升端到端自动驾驶系统的可解释性与鲁棒性。教师模型采用反思推理机制生成并优化逻辑解释，学生模型通过监督微调蒸馏其推理能力，并利用航点解码器将文本推理转化为连续轨迹。该框架在Waymo基准测试中显著优于传统方法，实现了零样本推理、轨迹精度和推理效率的提升，其中推理文本带来了约24%的性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型通常作为黑盒，在复杂场景中表现不佳；而现有引入视觉语言模型（VLMs）以提供明确推理的方法依赖于可能有误的预生成标注，且需要高昂的人工成本。因此，需要一种无需中间标签、能够增强零样本泛化能力和可解释性的新方法。

Method: 采用教师-学生架构。教师模型：利用视觉语言模型进行反思推理，即在真值动作监督下生成并迭代优化逻辑解释，以增强零样本泛化能力。学生模型：通过监督微调（SFT）蒸馏教师模型的推理能力。同时，设计了一个独立的航点解码器，用于将文本形式的推理转化为连续的驾驶轨迹。整个框架通过分层推理引擎整合明确推理与驾驶预测两个目标。

Result: 在Waymo基准测试上进行评估，本框架在零样本推理、航点准确性（轨迹精度）和推理效率方面均优于经典的基于推理的基线方法。实验证明，推理文本对驾驶推理过程贡献显著，与不具备推理能力的相同模型相比，性能提升约24%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过教师-学生架构成功整合了明确的可解释推理与稳健的驾驶性能，推动了自动驾驶系统向可解释、可部署的方向发展。其设计验证了结构化推理在提升自动驾驶性能与可解释性方面的关键作用。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving aims to learn a direct mapping from visual observations to control actions. However, these E2E models often act as black boxes and struggle with complex scenarios. To address this, recent works incorporate Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to provide explicit reasoning, enhancing both interpretability and driving robustness. These approaches typically rely on pre-generated annotations, which suffer from potentially flawed labels and require costly human labor. In this work, we propose a new framework that integrates structured reasoning and geometric precision through a teacher-student architecture. The teacher model introduces reflective reasoning, where the VLM generates logical explanations and then reflectively refines the reasoning under the supervision of ground-truth action. This enhances zero-shot generalization without intermediate labels. The student model distills the teacher's reasoning capabilities via supervised fine-tuning. We also design a separate waypoint decoder that interprets textual reasoning into continuous trajectories. Our proposed solution integrates two goals: providing explicit reasoning for interpretability and delivering robust and accurate driving performance. It leverages the synergy between these two objectives within a staged inference engine to enhance driving performance and explicitly uses the reasoning to guide driving prediction. Evaluated on Waymo benchmarks, our framework outperforms classical reasoning-based baselines in zero-shot reasoning, waypoint accuracy, and inference efficiency. Our experiments validate this design, demonstrating that the reasoning text makes a significant contribution to driving inference, resulting in around a 24% improvement in performance compared to an identical model that lacks reasoning. Our work advances reasoning-driven autonomous driving toward interpretable and deployable systems.

</details>


### [83] [VSTaI: Design and Characterization of Variable-Stiffness Tactile Interfaces Based on 3D-Printed Structured Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29102)
*Yiting Mo,Xinyuan Mao,Jashan Preet Singh,Fernando Bello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一种基于真空诱导结构化织物堵塞的紧凑型可变刚度触觉界面（VSTaI），用于实时模拟软组织刚度变化，以实现更逼真的触诊训练。通过真空调节内部压力，其刚度可从亚兆帕级别显著提升至兆帕级别，并具有良好的形状适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的触诊训练模拟器难以实时、可靠地渲染软组织在按压时的刚度变化，限制了训练的真实感。因此，需要开发一种能够动态调节刚度并能贴合复杂形状的新型触觉界面。

Method: 1. **界面设计**：VSTaI由一个真空密封的结构化织物层夹在两个硅胶层之间构成，通过调节内部真空压力来改变织物层的堵塞状态，从而调控整体刚度。2. **材料与结构**：使用3D打印技术制造了四种具有不同几何参数的结构化织物图案。3. **性能测试**：对每种图案在大气压（未堵塞）和真空（堵塞）条件下进行力-压痕测试，评估其刚度变化、模量、空间均匀性和形状适应性。

Result: 1. **刚度调节**：真空诱导的堵塞使所有测试图案的刚度均显著增加，有效模量从亚兆帕级提升到兆帕级。其中一种配置在堵塞状态下刚度增幅高达140%。2. **图案特性**：圆形锁子甲图案的触觉刚度空间分布最均匀；几何更致密的图案能达到更高的峰值刚度。3. **形状适应性**：VSTaI展现出优异的贴合底层几何形状的能力（共形性）。

Conclusion: 基于结构化织物的堵塞是一种实用且有效的技术路径，可用于制造形状可适应、刚度实时可调的触觉显示界面。该VSTaI设计特别适用于需要模拟软组织力学特性的医学触诊训练场景，能提升训练的真实感和效果。

Abstract: Realistic palpation training requires reliable rendering of soft tissue stiffness changes in real time, which is difficult to achieve with conventional simulators. This paper presents a compact, variable-stiffness tactile interface (VSTaI) based on vacuum-induced jamming of 3D-printed structured fabrics. A vacuum-sealed fabric layer is sandwiched between two silicone layers, and stiffness is tuned by regulating internal pressure. Four fabric patterns with different geometric parameters were fabricated and evaluated using force-indentation tests under atmospheric and vacuum conditions. Across the tested pattern and geometry combinations, vacuum jamming increased stiffness significantly, producing an effective modulus from sub-megapascal to megapascal levels. Specifically, one configuration exhibited a stiffness increase of up to 140% under the jammed state. Circular chainmail patterns provided the most spatially uniform distribution of tactile stiffness, while denser geometries reached higher peak stiffness. VSTaI was also shown to exhibit excellent conformability to the underlying geometry. These results support structured-fabric jamming as a practical approach for shape-conformable, tunable-stiffness displays aimed at physical examination training.

</details>

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation but remain vulnerable to runtime disturbances that break the temporal alignment among visual observations, robot states, and executed actions. We introduce ActFovea, a plug-and-play safeguarding framework that detects and mitigates such failures without retraining or modifying the underlying VLA policy. ActFovea uses robot kinematics, proprioceptive states, and recent actions to construct action-conditioned foveated regions that retain contact-relevant areas and predicted motion corridors while suppressing task-irrelevant visual content. It detects runtime risks by evaluating whether visual motion and observation freshness remain consistent with geometric, proprioceptive, and action transitions. For recoverable disturbances, ActFovea constructs disturbance-specific candidate observations and accepts a recovery only after verifying the resulting action chunk. When stale or replayed observations make reliable recovery impossible, it invokes a bounded safe-failure procedure. In closed-loop evaluations of $π_0$ across multiple LIBERO suites, ActFovea increases success under localized visual overlays from 49.3\% to 90.3\%, closing 93.7\% of the gap to clean performance. It further improves success under action drift and visual delay by 7.0 and 9.8 percentage points, respectively, while preserving clean-task performance. Under frozen-observation replay, ActFovea triggers timely safe failure in all trials, with no unprotected failures. These results demonstrate that spatiotemporal visual-action consistency provides an effective basis for runtime safeguarding of VLA policies.

</details>

*Yuxin Chen,Hari Srikanth,Nathan Jew,Menglin Wu,Pengcheng Wang,Junli Ren,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Peng Xu,Jinyu Xie,Thomas Tian*

Abstract: While robot foundation models are growing increasingly capable, the strongest models are typically trained on proprietary data and remain closed-source, limiting downstream users' ability to adapt them to new tasks, embodiments, and deployment settings. Following the LLM community, an emerging access paradigm for closed-weight robot foundation models is the managed supervised fine-tuning (SFT) API, where users submit training data and receive a tuned policy without access to model weights, gradients, or training internals. While such APIs let downstream users leverage powerful proprietary foundation models, they restrict policy improvement to pure imitation, ruling out reinforcement learning and other closed-loop methods that rely on internal training signals. This limitation is particularly acute for agile, contact-rich humanoid manipulation, where the gap between policy outputs and deployed behavior is large due to novel states, action tracking dynamics, latency, and controller-specific failure modes. We study how effective this managed-API regime is for humanoid adaptation, and how closed-loop improvement can be realized within it to push policies toward task mastery. We conduct one of the first empirical studies of managed-API adaptation on a real humanoid, instantiated on Gemini Robotics On-Device (GROD). We find that direct SFT through the API substantially outperforms a leading open-weight VLA trained on the same demonstrations, yet still falls short of deployment-level mastery on agile, contact-rich tasks. To close this gap, we introduce CLIFT: Closed-Loop Iterative Fine-Tuning, which turns deployment-time reward feedback into API-compatible supervised data and enables closed-loop policy improvement without accessing weights, gradients, likelihoods, or losses-pushing GROD to near-perfect success after two flywheel cycles, all without "opening the model box."

</details>


### [86] [MROPE: A Multi-Robot Safe Cooperative Strategy via combined Predictive Safety Filters and Ellipse-based Constraint Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29203)
*Alice Rosetti,Lorenzo Pichierri,Domenico Cappello,Fabrizio Schiano,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机集群在复杂环境中跟踪动态目标所面临的计算与安全性挑战，提出了一种名为MROPE的层次化策略。该方法通过将障碍物几何动态聚合为安全椭圆，并结合分布式优化、分散共识与预测安全滤波器，在保证实时避障的同时，实现了高效的集群协同，实验验证了其在实时性和可扩展性上优于集中式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集、杂乱的环境中部署无人机集群跟踪动态目标，面临严重的计算瓶颈（因环境复杂度导致规划问题维度高）和安全挑战（需确保集群内及与障碍物间的实时避障）。传统集中式方法难以满足实时性和可扩展性要求，因此需要一种能解耦高层任务与底层安全约束、并能高效处理复杂几何的分布式框架。

Method: 提出MROPE层次化策略：1) 动态聚合复杂障碍物几何为单个安全边界椭圆，以简化安全约束；2) 采用分布式聚合优化进行高层集群协调；3) 利用分散共识协议计算各无人机的安全区域（椭圆）；4) 在本地使用预测安全滤波器实现实时碰撞避免。该架构解耦了协同监测与局部安全需求。

Result: 通过虚拟仿真和真实世界实验验证了MROPE框架。结果表明，与集中式方法相比，MROPE具有显著的实时效率（计算速度快）和良好的可扩展性（无人机数量增加时性能下降平缓），同时能确保跟踪任务的有效执行和飞行安全。

Conclusion: MROPE框架通过层次化设计、几何聚合和分布式控制，有效解决了复杂环境中无人机集群跟踪的实时计算与安全性难题。该方法在保持任务性能的同时，大幅提升了系统的实时响应能力和可扩展性，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying drone swarms to track a dynamic target in cluttered environments presents severe computational and safety challenges. We propose MROPE, a hierarchical strategy that decouples the cooperative monitoring mission from strict local safety requirements. To overcome the computational bottlenecks typical of dense spaces, our approach dynamically aggregates complex obstacle geometries into a single safe bounding ellipse for each drone. Methodologically, this architecture is realized by combining distributed aggregative optimization for high-level swarm coordination, a decentralized consensus scheme for the safe area computation, and local Predictive Safety Filters (PSF) for real-time collision avoidance. Virtual and real-world experiments validate the framework, demonstrating superior real-time efficiency and scalability compared to centralized approaches.

</details>

Abstract: We present a real-time upper-body human-to-humanoid motion imitation framework driven by neuromorphic event-based vision. This work addresses practical perceptual bottlenecks of conventional frame-based RGB sensors, specifically their difficulty in high dynamic range (HDR) scenes and rapid motions due to fixed integration times. By leveraging the Prophesee EVK4 event camera, which operates asynchronously with high temporal resolution and a dynamic range exceeding 120 dB, our system supports stable tracking in conditions where standard vision pipelines degrade, such as severe backlighting and very low light environments below 5 lux. The architecture integrates a low-latency Perception Module, utilizing optimized event accumulation and gravity-aligned inertial fusion, with a causal Motion Module (TWIST) that performs online kinematic retargeting. We validate the system on an embedded NVIDIA Booster T1 platform and an 18-DoF humanoid upper-body setup, demonstrating an end-to-end photon-to-action latency of 23-34 ms and advantages over RGB baselines under our experimental setup. The results indicate a practical trade-off: events can be preferable for fast or poorly lit upper-body teleoperation, whereas well-lit static scenes may favor RGB or hybrid sensing.

### [88] [TacPrint: A Wearable Fingertip Tactile Sensor for Human-to-Robot Contact Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29231)
*Yongxi Liu,Chaofan Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Xiangyin Bao,Boyue Zhang,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacPrint是一种新型可穿戴指尖触觉传感器，通过硅胶凸起与24个电容触元一一对应，结合真实-模拟-真实流程从24通道信号估计35×26接触深度图。该传感器在精度评估和机器人任务（抓取、擦拭）中表现优异，显著提升了人机任务转换成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类中心数据收集对机器人技能获取至关重要，但现有低成本、可扩展的触觉传感系统难以在不干扰自然操作的情况下捕捉精细指尖交互，这降低了接触密集型任务中人机转换的可靠性。

Method: 1. 传感器设计：TacPrint采用可穿戴设计，硅胶皮肤内表面的凸起与24个电容式触元一一对应，实现局部电容响应。2. 数据处理流程：提出真实-模拟-真实（real-to-sim-to-real）流程，使用神经网络从24通道电容信号估计35×26的接触深度图。

Result: 1. 精度评估：在模拟生成的标签上，接触区域RMSE为0.223±0.161mm，加权质心误差为1.213±2.379像素，IoU为0.829±0.169。2. 实际测量：电容输入下，网络预测深度在引导校准接触中心的平均绝对误差为0.085±0.057mm；接触位置平均误差为0.250±0.208mm。3. 任务性能：在人类-机器人重放中，触觉引导补偿将抓取和擦拭成功率从0%分别提升至91.67%和90%。在闭环抓取中，深度反馈的成功率（所有测试位置87.5%，边缘接触85%）显著高于原始触元反馈（67.5%和45%）。

Conclusion: TacPrint提供了一种低成本、可扩展且不影响自然操作的可穿戴触觉传感解决方案，能准确估计指尖接触深度，显著提升接触密集型机器人任务（如抓取和擦拭）的成功率，有效改善人机任务转换性能。

Abstract: Human-centric data collection is emerging as a significant paradigm for robot skill acquisition, but seamlessly integrating low-cost, scalable tactile sensing systems that capture fine-grained fingertip interactions without compromising natural operation remains a key challenge. This reduces the reliability of human-to-robot transfer in contact-rich tasks. In this work, we present TacPrint, a wearable fingertip tactile sensor, where protrusions on the inner surface of the silicone skin are aligned one-to-one with 24 capacitive taxels to enable localized capacitive responses. A real-to-sim-to-real pipeline estimates a 35 $\times$ 26 contact-depth map from 24-channel capacitive signals. Against simulation-generated labels, the model achieved a contact-region RMSE of 0.223 $\pm$ 0.161 mm, a weighted-centroid error of 1.213 $\pm$ 2.379 pixels, and an IoU of 0.829 $\pm$ 0.169. With measured capacitive inputs, the network-predicted depth evaluated at the guide-calibrated contact center showed a mean absolute error of 0.085 $\pm$ 0.057 mm across all 40 controlled trials, while the mean contact-position error was 0.250 $\pm$ 0.208 mm across the 37 trials whose reference contact regions were not truncated by the sensing boundary. In human-to-robot replay, tactile-guided compensation increased grasping and wiping success rates from 0% to 91.67% and 90%, respectively. In closed-loop grasping, dense-depth feedback achieved success rates of 87.5% over all tested positions and 85% under edge-contact conditions, compared with 67.5% and 45% for raw-taxel feedback.

</details>


### [89] [FBFM: A Training-Free Asynchronous Feedback Mechanism for Flow-Matching in World-Action Models Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29235)
*Peize Li,Ruimeng Zhang,Ru Zhang,Cong Huang,Kai Chen,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Feedback Flow Matching (FBFM)，一种无需训练的推理机制，用于增强世界动作模型（WAMs）在长时域机器人控制任务中的可靠性和实时校正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）在长时域任务中依赖粗粒度的分块反馈机制，无法在单个时间步水平上及时纠正预测误差，导致在意外事件或误差累积时性能下降。

Method: 提出Feedback Flow Matching (FBFM)方法，在流动匹配推理过程中，应用掩膜伪逆校正到条件速度场上。具体而言，利用前一个动作块的反馈引导下一个动作块的生成，并结合执行前一个动作块后观察到的真实图像来引导下一帧的预测，形成一个异步的跨块校正循环。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboTwin2.0基准测试任务上，FBFM在有利设置下将任务成功率提升超过5%。在真实世界机器人实验中，观测预测诊断显示其追踪效果有显著改善。

Conclusion: FBFM提供了一种新的细粒度在线校正范式，有效弥合了开环流动生成与闭环现实世界动态之间的差距，提升了长时域机器人控制的可靠性和响应性。

Abstract: Although world-action models (WAMs) enhance long-horizon robot control by predicting visual evolution before acting, long-horizon reliability demands repeated re-grounding in real observations--not recursive rollout. Existing WAMs address this by refreshing history or KV cache with ground-truth data between chunks. However, such chunk-wise feedback operates at a coarse temporal granularity and thus fails to correct prediction errors at the individual time-step level. To address this, we propose Feedback Flow Matching (FBFM), a training-free inference mechanism that pushes re-grounding inside the actively generated chunk. During flow matching, FBFM applies a masked pseudoinverse correction to the conditional velocity field: it leverages the preceding action chunk to guide generation of the next action chunk, and uses the image observed after executing that preceding chunk to guide the next frame prediction. This cross-chunk pairing--where feedback from one chunk arrives in time to shape the next--creates an asynchronous loop that corrects errors without waiting for chunk boundaries. Being training-free, the mechanism improves responsiveness to unexpected events and suppresses drift in long-horizon tasks. We evaluate FBFM on both a joint-generation WAM (DreamZero) and a stage-wise WAM (LingBot-VA). On selected LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0 tasks, it improves success rates by over 5% in favorable settings, and real-world robot observation-prediction diagnostics show notably better tracking. We argue that FBFM offers a new paradigm for fine-grained online correction, bridging open-loop flow generation with closed-loop real-world dynamics.

</details>


### [90] [MDIR: A Task-Manifold Impedance Retargeting Method for Contact-Rich Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29271)
*Liu Jiahao,Kento Kawaharazuka,Tasuku Makabe,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为流形分解阻抗重定向（MDIR）的方法，用于解决固定阻抗遥操作在接触丰富任务中力冲击和变异性大的问题。该方法能从单次示教数据中自动生成可变阻抗控制命令，显著降低了操作过程中的激进性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的遥操作任务中，固定阻抗控制器虽然使示教变得实用，但其增益在确保任务进度和接触支持的同时，也导致了冲击力大和力变异性高的问题。现有方法通常需要多次示教或优化，本文旨在研究如何从单次示教数据中进行控制器到控制器的阻抗重定向，以改善力控性能。

Method: 本文提出了流形分解阻抗重定向（MDIR）方法。给定一个固定的笛卡尔阻抗命令序列 {K0, D0, xcmd}，MDIR通过以下步骤确定性地将其重参数化为可执行的可变阻抗命令：1）在任务通道中表示源响应（考虑工作、施力和支撑通道），并在控制链度量下保留被动残余互补；2）计算可执行的笛卡尔到流形重定向（C2M）基线；3）应用流形约束参数优化（MPO），以选择一个可行的代表，该代表具有更低的腕部力峰值、冲量、力变异性和标称控制器功率。

Result: 在Franka Panda机器人上进行的平面擦拭、抓取放置和推压三项任务的实验表明，完整的MDIR控制器在所有15次闭环执行中都通过了任务检查（Task Check）。与固定阻抗示教相比，MDIR控制器在所有四个激进性指标（腕部力峰值、冲量、力变异性、标称控制器功率）上均实现了降低。

Conclusion: MDIR方法能够从单次固定阻抗示教中有效生成可变阻抗控制策略，在不牺牲任务完成度的前提下，显著降低了接触过程中的力冲击和变异性，提升了遥操作的安全性和平顺性。

Abstract: Fixed Cartesian impedance makes contact-rich teleoperation demonstrations practical, but gains that secure progress and contact support also determine impact and force variability. We study single-demonstration controller-to-controller impedance retargeting. Given one fixed Cartesian impedance command sequence {K0, D0, xcmd}, Manifold-Decomposed Impedance Retargeting (MDIR) deterministically reparameterizes the recorded controller into an executable task-channel variable-impedance command. MDIR targets this local retargeting problem by preserving projected task-channel responses near the demonstrated trajectory. It represents the source response in operational work, exertion, and support channels with a passive residual complement under a control-chain metric, computes an executable Cartesian-to-Manifold Retargeting (C2M) baseline, and applies Manifold-Constrained Parameter Optimization (MPO) to select a feasible representative with lower wrist-force peaks, impulse, force variability, and nominal controller power. Across planar wiping, pick-and-place, and pushing on a Franka Panda, the full MDIR controller passes Task Check in all 15 closed-loop executions and reduces all four aggressiveness metrics relative to the fixed-impedance demonstrations.

</details>


### [91] [TRACT: Temporally Routed Action Chunks with Chronological Phase Authority for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29285)
*Jiahao Liu,Kento Kawaharazuka,Tasuku Makabe,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRACT方法，通过将阶段结构化的动作分块分解为当前阶段和未来动作中的单个边界，解决了机器人模仿学习中阶段边界跨越导致的时序不匹配问题。该方法在真实机器人任务中显著提升了成功率和任务完成度，并消除了阶段模糊和停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阶段条件化的动作分块在预测的未来动作跨越程序边界时，将当前阶段分配给整个分块会产生结构性的时序错配，导致机器人任务执行效率和成功率下降。因此，需要一种能够精确处理阶段边界和过渡的新方法。

Method: TRACT方法包含三个核心组件：1) 任务局部图限制时间阶段权威；2) 累积边界分布单调度路由未来查询；3) 因果响应缺陷积分器用于接触执行，通过比较策略意图与后续运动来累积补偿并衰减。该方法将阶段结构化的动作分块分解为接受的当前阶段和单个“当前到下一个”边界。

Result: 在六个真实机器人变体（每个10次试验）的评估中，完整TRACT方法实现了10/10的全序列成功，99.00%的中位擦拭完成度，零阶段模糊和零停滞。与其他方法对比：成功率从3/10提升至6/10，擦拭完成度从8.03%提升至77.08%；阶段权威减少阶段模糊从8/10至0/10；响应集成减少停滞从4/10至0/10。

Conclusion: TRACT通过精确管理阶段边界和过渡，有效解决了传统动作分块方法中的时序不匹配问题，显著提升了机器人模仿学习在真实接触任务中的性能、可靠性和鲁棒性。实验结果表明，其路由表示在观察到的任务结果上优于扁平包表示。

Abstract: Action chunking shortens the effective decision horizon of robot imitation learning by predicting multiple future actions, while conventional phase conditioning describes the current control instant. When a predicted horizon crosses a procedural boundary, assigning the current phase to the entire chunk creates a structural temporal mismatch. We present TRACT, which factorizes phase-structured action chunking into an accepted current phase and a single CURRENT-to-NEXT boundary inside the future horizon. A task-local graph constrains chronological phase authority, and a cumulative boundary distribution monotonically routes future queries through phase-specific query and action paths. For contact execution, a causal response-deficit integrator compares policy intent with ACK-eligible subsequent motion, accumulates arm compensation when directional response is suppressed, and decays after confirmed recovery. Across six real-robot variants with ten trials each, full TRACT achieves 10/10 full-sequence success, 99.00 [88.75, 100.00]% median [min, max] wipe completion, zero observed phase ambiguity, and zero stalls. Under the current complete method package and evaluation setting, the routed representation obtains better observed task results than the flat package (6/10 vs. 3/10 success; 77.08% vs. 8.03% median wipe completion). Chronological authority reduces observed phase ambiguity from 8/10 to 0/10, and response integration reduces stalls from 4/10 to 0/10. The package comparison does not isolate routing from other generator-package differences.

</details>


### [92] [BWM: A Low-Cost High-Fidelity World Simulator for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29302)
*BWM Team*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BWM是一个开源、低成本、高保真的动作条件世界模拟器，用于机器人操作，通过结合初始环境指导、动态视觉历史和时间对齐的动作条件进行有状态自回归预测，在模拟保真度和功能实用性上优于现有方法，并在WorldArena挑战赛中总体排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理模拟器需要大量资产构建和校准，且存在模拟与现实（sim-to-real）的差距；视频生成器则缺乏对细粒度机器人动作的精确控制。因此，亟需一种能够可靠预测动作后果（包括风险和故障）的高保真、可控的世界模拟器，以支持机器人学习。

Method: 提出了Boundless World Model（BWM），一个动作条件世界模型。其核心方法是结合初始环境指导、动态视觉历史和时间对齐的机器人动作条件，进行有状态的自回归未来观察预测。通过轨迹回放、重叠剪辑采样和初始观察增强技术构建动作对齐的训练数据。BWM可作为数据引擎（通过动作对齐的回放增强模仿学习数据）和策略评估器（用于闭环评估、风险预测和策略排名）。

Result: 在WorldArena基准和物理机器人上的实验表明，BWM显著提升了模拟器的保真度（模拟真实性）和功能实用性（作为数据引擎和评估器的有效性）。在WorldArena挑战赛中，BWM在Track 1及其两个Track 2应用中均获得总体排名第一。

Conclusion: BWM是一个有效的开源世界模拟器，为机器人操作提供了高保真、可控的预测环境，能够可靠地生成训练数据和评估策略，成功解决了现有模拟器的局限性。其开源生态系统（包括模型、代码和接口）将促进机器人学习研究的发展。

Abstract: Reliable robot learning requires a world simulator that can predict action consequences before execution on physical hardware, including risky and failure-prone outcomes. Existing physics simulators require substantial asset construction and calibration and still face a sim-to-real gap, while video generators often lack precise control over their responses to fine-grained robot actions. In this paper, we present the Boundless World Model (BWM), an open-source, low-cost, high-fidelity world simulator for robot manipulation. BWM is an action-conditioned world model that combines initial-environment guidance, dynamic visual history, and temporally aligned robot-action conditioning for stateful autoregressive prediction of future observations. We construct action-aligned training clips through trajectory replay, overlapping clip sampling, and initial-observation enhancement. BWM serves as a data engine that augments imitation-learning data with action-aligned rollouts, and as a policy evaluator for closed-loop assessment, risk anticipation, and policy ranking. Experiments on the WorldArena benchmark and physical robots demonstrate improved simulator fidelity and functional utility across the data-engine and policy-evaluator settings. BWM ranks first overall in the WorldArena Challenge across Track 1 and its two Track 2 applications. We release the BWM open-source ecosystem, including model checkpoints, training and inference code, and interfaces for data generation and policy evaluation.

</details>


### [93] [SAGP: Semantic Affordance-Guided Grasp Planning via Coarse-Zone VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29374)
*Muhayy Ud Din,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGP：一个无需训练、基于粗粒度区域抽象的抓取规划框架，通过结合视觉语言模型的语义推理与几何规划，解决了传统方法产生功能不合理抓取的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于几何的抓取规划器能保证物理有效性，但忽略了功能语义，常产生在实际中不恰当的抓取（如抓杯沿、刀刃），导致下游任务失败。现有的视觉语言模型方法要么依赖精细的类别特定分割，要么直接推断抓取姿态易产生空间幻觉。目前尚缺乏一个实用、无需训练的框架，能稳健地将高层语义推理与几何抓取规划联系起来。

Method: 提出Semantic Affordance-Guided Grasp Planning (SAGP)，一个无需训练的流程，其核心是一个“粗粒度区域抽象层”。该方法首先通过基于PCA对齐和距离驱动的DBSCAN聚类，将物体点云划分为空间区域（顶部、中部、底部、侧面、突起），完全绕过学习到的分割。然后，一个预训练的VLM通过结构化的零样本查询评估每个区域的抓取质量。最后，将区域评分与几何、可达性和任务对齐信号融合，以重新排序对偶抓取候选。

Result: 在PyBullet仿真环境中使用Franka Panda机器人和YCB物体进行的实验表明，SAGP在保持纯几何规划高成功率的同时，显著提高了所选拓取的功能合理性，特别是在非对称、有手柄的物体上，这些物体仅靠几何信息是不足的。

Conclusion: 引入的粗粒度区域抽象方法，提供了一个有效、无需训练的桥梁，将基于VLM的推理与几何抓取规划连接起来，且无需进行精细的部件分割。

Abstract: Geometry-based grasp planners ensure physically valid grasps but ignore functional semantics, often generating grasps that are antipodal and collision-free yet practically inappropriate, for example, gripping a mug by its rim, a knife by the blade, or a bottle near its cap. These inconsistencies cause the downstream task to fail even when traditional grasp metrics are met. Existing vision-language model (VLM) approaches either depend on fine-grained, category-specific part segmentation or attempt to directly infer grasp poses, with the latter prone to spatial hallucinations. As a result, no practical, training-free framework has yet been proposed that robustly links high-level semantic reasoning to geometric grasp planning. We introduce Semantic Affordance-Guided Grasp Planning (SAGP), a training-free pipeline built on a coarse-zone abstraction layer. The method first partitions the object point cloud into spatial regions (top, middle, bottom, lateral sides, and protrusions) by applying PCA-based alignment followed by distance-driven DBSCAN clustering, entirely bypassing learned segmentation. A pre-trained VLM then assesses the grasp quality of each region through a structured zero-shot query, and the resulting zone-wise scores are fused with geometric, reachability, and task-alignment signals to re-rank antipodal grasp candidates. Experiments on YCB objects in PyBullet with a Franka Panda robot show that SAGP preserves the high success rate of geometry-only planning while substantially improving the functional appropriateness of selected grasps, particularly on asymmetric, handle-bearing objects where geometry alone is uninformative. The introduced coarse-zone abstraction offers an effective, training-free bridge between VLM-based reasoning and geometric grasp planning, without the need for fine-grained part segmentation.

</details>


### [94] [AquaJEPA: Action-Conditioned Multimodal Predictive Representations for Underwater Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29393)
*Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Alexey Gavrilov,Sergey Muravyov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaJEPA是一种用于水下机器人的动作条件联合嵌入预测模型，通过融合RGB相机、前视声纳和本体感觉传感器来应对传感器可靠性随环境变化的问题。该模型在仿真环境中优于多种基准方法，实现了最佳的闭环任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人的传感器（如相机和声纳）的可靠性会随着水下能见度、视角和车辆运动而发生剧烈变化。为了提升在复杂和动态变化的水下环境中的自主导航和任务执行能力，需要开发一种能够显式处理传感器有效性变化、并融合多模态感知信息的预测与规划方法。

Method: 研究提出了AquaJEPA模型，这是一个动作条件联合嵌入预测模型。其核心方法包括：1）显式融合RGB相机、前视声纳和本体感觉数据，并包含传感器有效性掩码；2）基于八推进器控制命令预测未来的潜在状态目标；3）同时预测速度和声纳轮廓，供共享的滚动时域规划器使用。研究在Stonefish仿真平台中，通过系统性地消融关键组件（如EMA目标、动作边界、掩码和模态丢弃）来评估模型性能。

Result: 在涵盖多种障碍地图、水能见度系数和动力学条件的120个仿真环境中进行评估，AquaJEPA模型在DVL观测间歇丢失的情况下，成功到达74个目标点（基准方法为68个），并取得了最低的平均最终定位误差（0.906米）。与普通多模态预测、监督动力学模型和递归世界模型这三个基于动作预测的基准方法相比，AquaJEPA在配对最终误差上分别实现了0.273米、0.364米和0.106米的显著降低。

Conclusion: AquaJEPA模型通过其独特的动作条件联合嵌入预测架构，有效融合了多模态传感器信息并应对了传感器有效性的动态变化，从而在水下机器人闭环控制任务中实现了最优的综合性能。实验结果表明，它显著优于其他几种先进的动作条件预测模型，但其相对于仅使用状态信息（state-only）方法的统计显著性优势仍有待进一步明确。

Abstract: Underwater robots combine complementary sensors whose reliability changes abruptly with water visibility, viewpoint, and vehicle motion. We introduce AquaJEPA, an action-conditioned joint-embedding predictive model that fuses an RGB camera, forward-looking sonar, and proprioception with explicit sensor validity. It predicts a future latent target conditioned on eight-thruster commands and supplies velocity and sonar-profile predictions to a shared receding-horizon planner. We study the method in Stonefish against reactive, state-only, ordinary multimodal, supervised dynamics, and recurrent world-model baselines. We further isolate the EMA target, action margin, masks, and modality dropout. A preregistered 120-environment replication comprises five independent replicates of a grid crossing three unseen obstacle maps, four water-visibility coefficients, and nominal versus shifted dynamics, while intermittently removing DVL observations. In 120 fresh paired environments with scheduled DVL loss, AquaJEPA reaches 74 goals, versus 68 for both state-only and the recurrent world model, and attains the lowest mean final error (0.906 m). Paired final-error reductions relative to ordinary multimodal prediction, supervised dynamics, and the recurrent world model are 0.273 m (95% CI: 0.190-0.356), 0.364 m (0.260-0.468), and 0.106 m (0.025-0.187), respectively. AquaJEPA therefore achieves the best aggregate closed-loop performance and significantly outperforms three action-conditioned predictive baselines in paired final error; its advantage over state-only remains statistically unresolved.

</details>


### [95] [Automated Straight-line Sewing of Stretchable Fabrics with Different Lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29464)
*Bingchen Jin,Akinari Kobayashi,Dipankar Bhattacharya,Akira Seino,Fuyuki Tokuda,Norman Chihnan Tien,Kazuhiro Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究针对自动化缝纫中不同长度布料对齐缝合（DLAS）的挑战，提出了一种名为DLRoSS的新型机器人缝纫系统。该系统由安装在六自由度机械臂上的滚轮型末端执行器构成，通过四个自动化阶段（布料包裹、缝纫、缝纫完成、布料释放）实现了对不同长度弹性布料的高质量自动对齐缝合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化缝纫过程中，对不同长度的布料进行对齐缝合（DLAS）是一项需要满足多项要求的复杂任务。为了解决这一挑战，实现高质量、自动化的DLAS过程，有必要开发一种新的系统。

Method: 本文提出的DLRoSS系统由一个六自由度机械臂和一个滚轮型末端执行器组成。该末端执行器包含主动短布料滚轮、主动长布料滚轮以及一个安装在短布料滚轮上的被动压轮。系统假设两层布料的一端已初始定位于缝纫机压脚下，随后通过四个阶段自动化完成DLAS：(P1) 布料包裹：拾取、夹持并从另一端将布料包裹到送料滚轮上；(P2) 缝纫：在缝纫过程中，基于缝纫速度和已知的布料长度比，通过实时控制滚轮速度来拉伸短布料并与长布料对齐；(P3) 缝纫完成：在滚轮上进行最后一圈缝纫时，启动压轮以防止被拉伸的布料因内部张力而滑脱；(P4) 布料释放：缝纫结束时，布料边缘移过压轮后从滚轮上释放。

Result: 实验结果表明，DLRoSS系统能够对不同材质和长度的弹性布料实现一致、高质量的缝纫，验证了其在解决DLAS自动化难题上的有效性。

Conclusion: DLRoSS系统成功实现了对不同长度布料的自动化对齐缝合，证明了该机器人缝纫方案在应对DLAS挑战方面的可行性和有效性，为自动化纺织制造提供了一种新的技术方案。

Abstract: Different Length Alignment Sewing (DLAS), which involves stretching the shorter fabric to match the longer one and sewing them together in a straight line, is a challenging task that needs to satisfy several requirements when automating the sewing process. To address the challenges, this research proposes a novel robotic sewing system, Different Length Robotic Sewing System (DLRoSS), which consists of a roller type end-effector, attached to a 6-DoF manipulator. The end-effector composed of active shorter and longer fabric rollers, and a passive press-roller attached to the shorter-fabric roller. Assuming that one end of the two fabric layers are initially positioned under the sewing machine's presser foot, the system automates DLAS by operating in four distinct phases. (P1) Fabric wrapping: Individual fabric layers are picked, held, and wrapped from the other end onto the feed rollers. (P2) Sewing: During the sewing, the shorter fabric is stretched and aligned with the longer fabric in real-time using roller velocity control based on the sewing speed and apriori known length ratio. (P3) Sewing completion: In the final sewing round on the fabric rollers, the press roller is engaged to prevent the stretched fabric from slipping off due to internal tension. (P4) Sewing fabric release: At the end of sewing, the fabric edge moves past the press roller, and the fabric releases from the rollers. Experimental results demonstrate that DLRoSS achieves consistent, high-quality sewing of stretchable fabrics of different materials and lengths.

</details>


### [96] [Temporal Policy: History-Initialized Action Generation for Robotic Learning from Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29482)
*Dylan Miller,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Temporal Policy的生成框架，该框架基于随机插值器，通过将动作生成问题形式化为时间耦合的传输问题，显著降低了机器人学习示教中生成模型的计算复杂度和推理延迟，实现了高频闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的扩散模型和流匹配模型依赖于非信息性高斯先验的独立耦合，这迫使模型必须学习复杂且高成本的向量场来映射到物理动作空间，导致推理成本高昂，难以满足机器人高频闭环控制的需求。

Method: 提出了一种基于随机插值器的生成框架Temporal Policy。其核心是将动作生成重新定义为一个时间耦合的传输问题。具体做法是以机器人的近期状态历史作为生成流的起点，从而显式地将过去的状态与未来的动作序列耦合起来。这种数据依赖的耦合方式能够生成更直的向量场，从而降低传输成本。

Result: 在视觉运动仿真基准和物理机器人遥操作平台上的实验表明，与基于噪声初始化的基线模型相比，该方法将传输成本降低了近一个数量级，并在单NVIDIA RTX 4080 GPU上实现了19.1毫秒的低推理延迟。同时，其任务成功率与现有最先进的基线模型相当。

Conclusion: Temporal Policy通过引入时间耦合的传输机制，有效规避了独立高斯先验带来的计算瓶颈，在保持高性能的同时大幅提升了计算效率，为实现高频、实时的机器人闭环控制提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: By relying on independent couplings from uninformative Gaussian priors, standard diffusion and flow matching models are forced to learn complex, high-cost vector fields to reach the physical action space. Generative models excel at capturing multimodal behaviors for robotic Learning from Demonstration (LfD), but often suffer from high inference cost. This paper introduces Temporal Policy, a generative framework based on stochastic interpolants that formulates action generation as a temporally coupled transport problem. By initializing the generative flow at the robot's recent history, we explicitly couple past states to future action sequences. This data-dependent coupling reduces transport cost and produces straight vector fields. We validate Temporal Policy across visuomotor simulation benchmarks and on a physical Barrett WAM 2x 7DoF teleoperation platform. Our approach reduces transport costs by nearly an order of magnitude compared to noise-initialized baselines, achieving a 19.1 ms inference latency on a single NVIDIA RTX 4080. Crucially, these geometric and computational efficiencies are achieved while matching the success rates of state-of-the-art baselines. This simplified transport geometry bypasses the computational bottleneck of independent Gaussian priors, helping enable high-frequency, closed-loop control. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/dmiller12/TemporalPolicy.

</details>


### [97] [Tri-Space Operational Control of Redundant Multilink and Hybrid Cable-Driven Parallel Robots Using an Iterative-Learning based Reactive Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29500)
*Dipankar Bhattacharya,Yin Pok Chan,Siqi Shang,Yuen Shan Chan,Ying Tan,Darwin Lau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合反应控制（RC）和迭代学习控制（ILC）的缆索驱动并联机器人（CDPRs）三空间控制框架，用于执行重复性任务，并通过仿真和实验验证了其在多种CDPRs架构上的实时控制有效性和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆索驱动并联机器人（CDPRs）具有两级冗余和众多约束，现有方法缺乏一个鲁棒、有效且能直接应用于多种冗余驱动CDPRs架构的三空间（驱动、关节、操作空间）控制框架，以同时满足各空间约束并跟踪轨迹。

Method: 提出一个融合反应控制（RC）和迭代学习控制（ILC）的三空间控制框架；通过一种新颖的零空间向量参数化方法，在零空间中寻找最优参数，以在任务重复执行时通过ILC提升性能。

Result: 在多连杆缆索驱动机器人（MCDRs）和混合缆索驱动机器人（HCDRs）上进行的仿真和硬件实验表明，该框架可以方便、有效地应用于不同CDPRs的实时控制，能够在线跟踪操作空间轨迹，同时满足可行的缆索力并避免缆索-连杆干扰、关节干扰和操作性损失等不良情况。

Conclusion: 所提出的三空间控制框架能够在线跟踪操作空间轨迹并满足三空间约束，适用于重复性任务，通过零空间优化提升了控制性能，具有鲁棒性和广泛的适用性，为多种冗余驱动缆索驱动并联机器人的实时控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Cable-Driven Parallel Robots (CDPRs) are a type of parallel mechanism in which cables are used as actuators. Due to the two levels of redundancy and numerous constraints within the CDPR actuation, joint and operational spaces (together known as the tri-space), tracking a given trajectory in the operational space while satisfying constraints in tri-space simultaneously is challenging. To the best of the authors' knowledge, there does not exist any tri-space control framework, which is robust, effective, and directly applicable to several architectures of redundantly actuated CDPRs. This paper proposes a tri-space control framework that combines Reactive Control (RC) and Iterative-Learning Control (ILC) to perform repetitive tasks in the operational space. The framework allows the tracking of operational space trajectories online with feasible cable forces, while avoiding undesirable situations such as cable-link interference, joint interference, and loss of manipulability. On the other hand, by finding an optimal parameter in the null space using a novel parameterization of a null space vector, the performance can be improved through ILC when the task is repeatedly executed. Simulation and hardware results on various Multilink Cable-Driven Robot (MCDRs) and Hybrid Cable-Driven Robots (HCDRs) show that the proposed tri-space control framework can be conveniently and effectively applied to the real-time control of different CDPRs.

</details>


### [98] [Homotopy-Aware Corridor Generation without Predefined Reference Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29513)
*Haoze Dong,Minghan Li,Meng Guo,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于凸集图的无参考路径自适应多尺度无障碍走廊生成框架，用于机器人运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多依赖于预定义的参考路径，这会影响走廊几何形状，并隐性地限制了可探索的同伦类，从而可能阻碍规划最优解的发现。

Method: 1. 提出一个基于凸集图的无参考路径走廊生成框架，直接构建凸集序列作为走廊，使其从自由空间表示中自然形成。2. 将基于可视性的路径变形方法扩展到凸集序列，以识别和融合拓扑冗余的走廊，同时保留不同的替代方案。3. 开发了一种自适应多尺度GCS，包括支持局部更新的细粒度采样图和实现紧凑全局探索的粗粒度可视性图，两者保持拓扑一致性以支持增量更新。

Result: 数值实验表明，该框架能高效构建图结构，在轨迹级性能上表现稳定，并且生成的同伦感知轨迹持续时间短于现有基线。在地面和机载定位的无人机平台上的硬件实验，进一步验证了该框架在面对移动和未知障碍物时的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的无参考路径、自适应多尺度GCS框架，能够生成更灵活、拓扑多样且能适应环境变化的无障碍走廊，显著提升了机器人运动规划的效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generating safe corridors is essential for collision-free robotic motion planning, yet most existing methods rely on predefined reference paths, which bias corridor geometry and implicitly limit the homotopy classes that can be explored. We propose a reference-path-free corridor generation framework on graphs of convex sets (GCS) that constructs corridors directly as sequences of convex sets, allowing corridor structure to emerge from the free-space representation rather than from a guiding path. To reason about similarity among corridors, we extend visibility-based deformation from paths to convex-set sequences, enabling the fusion of topologically redundant corridors while preserving distinct alternatives. To overcome the limited adaptability of existing GCS methods based on static global decompositions, we further develop an adaptive multi-scale GCS, in which a sampling-based fine-scale graph supports localized updates and a visibility-based coarse-scale graph enables compact global exploration. The two levels maintain topological consistency, allowing incremental updates without full graph reconstruction under environmental uncertainty. Numerical experiments characterize GCS construction, corridor generation, homotopy-aware exploration, and local updates, showing efficient graph construction, stable trajectory-level performance, and shorter-duration homotopy-aware trajectories than existing baselines. Hardware experiments on ground and aerial robots, including deployment with onboard localization, further validate the framework under translated and previously unknown obstacles.

</details>


### [99] [STAGE: STyle-controllable Action GEneration for personalized autonomous driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29517)
*Zihao Liu,Xing Liu,Yizhai Zhang,Panfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为STAGE的风格可控动作生成方法，使自动驾驶系统能够根据用户偏好生成符合其驾驶风格的行为，通过模仿学习和偏好学习实现风格的可定制化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工业自动驾驶系统难以匹配个体驾驶员的多样化驾驶风格偏好，用户期望系统行为与自身习惯一致，因此需要一种能够实现风格可控的自动驾驶技术。

Method: STAGE方法基于模仿学习，结合风格值和潜在值进行动作模态编码；使用偏好学习识别用户驾驶风格为连续单调的风格值；开发规则降低偏好训练的人工成本；在推理阶段用户输入风格值以控制生成的动作模式。

Result: 在多个典型道路场景中验证，STAGE生成的风格可控动作与人类期望高度一致；与其他方法对比显示STAGE具备独特的风格可控性、风格连续性、驾驶风格对齐能力和驾驶安全性。

Conclusion: STAGE有效解决了自动驾驶系统风格匹配问题，通过可定制的风格值控制提升了用户体验，为开发更人性化的自动驾驶系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Driving style refers to the behavioral preferences that drivers maintain during driving, shaped by their diverse experiences, habits, and needs, and is typically reflected in varying levels of aggressiveness. If humans choose to use autonomous driving systems, they would expect the driving style of the systems to closely resemble their own habit. However, this is challenging for current industrial autonomous driving systems. To address this, we developed a style controllable action generation method, STAGE, for driving tasks. Its training process is based on imitation learning, incorporating both style value and latent value action modality encoding. Preference learning is then used to identify the user's driving style as a continuous, monotonic style value. And to reduce the cost of human involvement in the preference training process, we also developed a set of rules to compare driving style in data pairs. Then, during inference, the user inputs the style value to control the generated action patterns, dynamically meeting the user's expectations. Using the STAGE method, we verified that the style-controlled action generation results in several typical road scenarios significantly align with human expectations. Furthermore, through comparisons between the STAGE method and various other approaches, we reveal the unique functionalities of STAGE, including its style controllability, style continuity, driving style alignment capability and driving safety. The code for this work is available at: https://github.com/CarlDegio/STAGE

</details>


### [100] [TransGraspNet: Physically and Geometrically Consistent Manipulation of Transparent Labware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29567)
*Hailing Hu,Mingyi Zhu,Yiquan An,Yifei Tian,Tianyou Zuo,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TransGraspNet是一个用于透明玻璃器皿操作的几何物理一致性框架，通过确保边界、表面和物理一致性来提高抓取成功率和防止液体溢出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作透明实验室玻璃器皿具有固有的安全关键性，微小的几何误差会导致不稳定抓取和危险溢出。尽管在透明物体感知和机器人抓取方面取得了进展，但大多数现有系统独立优化检测、深度重建和抓取规划，导致跨阶段不一致：不完美的边界导致深度出血、扭曲的表面破坏法线估计、任务无关的抓取评分产生倾斜或偏离中心的抓取，在动态运动中失效。

Method: 提出了TransGraspNet框架，这是一个几何物理一致性框架，通过三个耦合原则显式地强制从感知到执行的一致性：1) 边界一致性：产生结构可靠的物体轮廓作为下游先验；2) 表面一致性：在深度重建中保持几何保真度和表面法线精度；3) 物理一致性：通过质心对齐和力矩空间稳定性优化抓取选择，以实现直立和动态稳健的操作。

Result: 在公共基准、专门的透明玻璃器皿数据集和真实机器人平台上进行了评估。结果显示边界质量和表面法线保真度得到改善，并在杂乱的透明场景中展示了强大的任务级性能。最重要的是，所提出的系统实现了可靠的现实世界操作，包括在杂乱环境中的高抓取成功率和在高速液体运输中的零溢出。

Conclusion: TransGraspNet通过显式强制从感知到执行的一致性，有效解决了透明玻璃器皿操作中的安全关键问题，实现了高抓取成功率和零溢出的可靠操作。

Abstract: Manipulating transparent laboratory glassware that contains liquid is inherently safety-critical: even small geometric errors can cause unstable grasps and hazardous spillage. Although recent progress has been made in transparent object perception and robotic grasping, most existing systems optimize detection, depth reconstruction, and grasp planning independently, which leads to cross-stage inconsistency imperfect boundaries induce depth bleeding, distorted surfaces corrupt normal estimation, and task agnostic grasp scoring yields tilted or off-center grasps that fail under dynamic motion. In this paper, we propose TransGraspNet, a geometry physics consistent framework that explicitly enforces consistency from perception to execution through three coupled principles: boundary consistency to produce structurally reliable object contours as downstream priors, surface consistency to preserve geometric fidelity and surface normal accuracy during depth reconstruction, and physics consistency to refine grasp selection with centroid alignment and wrench-space stability for upright and dynamically robust manipulation. We evaluate TransGraspNet on public benchmarks, a dedicated transparent glassware dataset, and a real robotic platform. The results show improved boundary quality and surface normal fidelity, and demonstrate strong task-level performance in cluttered transparent scenes. Most importantly, the proposed system achieves reliable real-world operation, including high grasp success rates in clutter and zero spillage during high speed liquid transport, highlighting the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [101] [Safe Vision Language Action Models via Barrier Enhanced Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29569)
*Kasra Sinaei,Hung-Chieh Wu,Donald Ebeigbe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化推理框架，将流匹配生成模型与控制屏障函数安全保证集成，在模型的去噪过程中内在地生成安全轨迹，无需外部过滤器、安全数据集或模型重训练，实现了高效、可靠的安全推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全生成方法通常依赖外部安全过滤器，这可能带来额外的计算开销并改变模型原始意图。研究动机在于开发一种能在生成过程中内在保证安全的模型框架，无需依赖特定安全数据集或昂贵的模型再训练，从而提供更通用、高效的安全推理解决方案。

Method: 提出一种模块化推理框架，核心是修改流匹配模型的去噪过程。通过引入平滑的Log-Sum-Exponential聚合屏障函数，在整个动作块（action chunks）上施加安全约束，从而确保生成轨迹的内在安全性。该方法理论上保证生成分布与目标分布之间的2-Wasserstein距离有界，并在不显著增加计算负担的前提下保持模型原始语义。

Result: 在两个机器人操作平台和一个2D导航基准测试上进行了实验验证。结果表明，该框架能够生成可靠的、符合安全约束的轨迹，并且不会降低模型在任务上的原有成功率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的集成流匹配与控制屏障函数的框架，为安全生成模型推理提供了一种通用且实用的解决方案。它无需专门的安全数据集或模型重训练，通过内在地约束生成过程，在保持计算效率和模型意图的同时，实现了可验证的安全保障。

Abstract: This article presents a modular inference framework that integrates Flow Matching generative models with formal Control Barrier Function (CBF) safety guarantees. Unlike existing methods that apply external safety filters to a model's final output, our approach modifies the Flow Matching denoising process within the model to inherently generate safe trajectories. By employing a smooth Log-Sum-Exponential aggregate barrier, we enforce safety over entire action chunks. This aggregate barrier ensures a minimal increase in computational overhead and does not alter the semantic intent of the model. We show that, within the proposed framework, the 2-Wasserstein distance between the generated distribution and the target distribution remains bounded. Our method eliminates the need for safety-specific datasets or costly model retraining, providing a versatile solution for safe inference. We validate the approach on two robotic manipulation platforms and a 2D navigation benchmark, verifying that our framework achieves reliable safety without degrading the success rate of the model.

</details>


### [102] [FibVLA: An Efficient Temporal Vision-Language-Action Model with Fibonacci Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29596)
*Li Lin,Wujun Xu,Weiwei Meng,Kaiwen Xia,Kang Hao Cheong,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FibVLA是一个高效处理长上下文历史的视觉语言动作模型框架，通过创新的时间感知技术解决了时间信息捕获与推理效率的平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言动作模型（VLAs）在捕获长上下文历史时间信息时存在效率下降问题，需要找到一种能够平衡时间信息捕获与推理效率的解决方案，以更好地支持具身AI应用。

Method: 1. 采用对数后视采样技术，对本体感觉状态和视觉帧进行采样，以最小冗余捕获长期时间依赖关系；2. 为动作专家引入流匹配技术生成动作分布；3. 采用斐波那契递归推理策略，基于实时闭环反馈生成长范围规划步骤。

Result: 实验证明，FibVLA在无需重新训练大型视觉编码器的情况下，显著提高了动作平滑度和成功率；效率分析显示，与基于视频的基线方法相比，在实时评估中具有优越的实时响应性。

Conclusion: FibVLA成功解决了视觉语言动作模型中时间信息捕获与推理效率之间的矛盾，为具身AI应用提供了一种高效、具备长上下文历史感知能力的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs), which leverage the cognition of multimodal information to infer physical-world actions, provide a generalized solution for embodied AI applications. Conventional VLAs usually concentrate on current digital cognition. While some efforts are made to enhance VLAs' reasoning capabilities by capturing temporal information, encoding the long-context history causes an efficiency-decreasing issue. To reconcile the conflict between capturing temporal information and maintaining inference efficiency in VLAs, this paper introduces FibVLA, an efficient framework featuring temporal perception of long-context history. Specifically, we leverage logarithmic hindsight sampling to both proprioceptive states and visual frames to capture long-term temporal dependencies with minimal redundancy. For the action expert, we introduce the flow matching to produce action distributions, and the Fibonacci recurrent inference strategy to generate long-range planning steps based on real-time closed-loop feedback. Experiments demonstrate that FibVLA significantly improves action smoothness and success rates without retraining large-scale visual encoders. Efficiency analysis demonstrates superior real-time responsiveness compared to video-based baselines in real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [103] [HAM-VLN: Harnessing Hierarchical Agentic Memory for Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29600)
*An Liu,Bingxi Liu,Hongyu Ding,Yixuan Jiang,Yaran Chen,Fulin Tang,Cong Leng,Hong Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HAM-VLN通过创建决策耦合的智能体记忆系统，为机器人提供持久的、基于深度的世界图，解决了视觉与语言导航中长时程导航的记忆与推理瓶颈，在无需额外训练的情况下，显著提升了导航成功率并大幅减少了超过65%的上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉与语言导航方法依赖于图像流或密集地图，在处理长时程导航任务时，不可避免地面临日益增长的记忆与推理瓶颈，限制了其在复杂未知环境中的性能与效率。

Method: 提出HAM-VLN，一种决策耦合的、由智能体自主构建的记忆机制。该机制在同一个模型调用中同时完成下一步动作选择和记忆记录，包括房间类型、物体、导航进度和失败笔记等语义及反思信息。近期的路径点被完整保留在一个有限窗口内，而较早的历史信息则通过基于相关性、新近性和显著性的检索机制，并结合一跳拓扑扩展的方式重新进入决策上下文。该设计无需在每个路径点决策之外增加额外的大语言模型调用。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，无需任何训练的HAM-VLN实现了优异的性能：在VLN-CE R2R上成功率(SR)达到61.0%，在VLN-CE RxR上达到52.7%，在HM3D-v2 ObjectNav上达到79.7%。同时，与先前方法相比，该方法将所需的上下文长度减少了超过65%。

Conclusion: HAM-VLN通过其创新的决策耦合记忆机制，不仅有效解决了长时程导航中的记忆瓶颈，显著提升了导航任务的成功率，还大幅降低了模型的上下文依赖长度，在性能与效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) enables robots to follow instructions in previously unseen environments. Recently, a training-free paradigm has emerged: the robot queries a multimodal LLM to understand its observations and plan the next action. However, long-horizon navigation based on either image streams or dense map inevitably introduces a growing memory and reasoning bottleneck. We present HAM-VLN, a decision-coupled, agent-authored memory that equips the robot with a persistent, depth-grounded world graph. In the same model call used to select the next action, HAM-VLN also records semantic and reflective information---including room type, objects, navigation progress, and failure notes. Recent waypoints remain verbatim within a bounded window, while older history re-enters the context only through retrieval scored by relevance, recency, and salience, together with one-hop topological expansion. This design requires no additional LLM calls beyond the per-waypoint decision. Compared to previous methods, HAM-VLN not only improves various navigation metrics but also reduces the context length by more than 65%. Specifically, HAM-VLN achieves 61.0% Success Rate (SR) on VLN-CE R2R, 52.7% SR on VLN-CE RxR, and 79.7% SR on HM3D-v2 ObjectNav without any training.

</details>


### [104] [WCM: A World Critic Model for Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29613)
*Senyu Fei,Xiaopeng Yu,Siyin Wang,Xianzhong Zhao,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出世界批评家模型（WCM），通过联合学习未来状态预测和价值估计，解决机器人操控中视觉-语言-动作模型强化学习后训练的关键挑战，显著提升性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操控的强化学习后训练中，批评家方法主要依赖单帧观测或潜在表示，与机器人控制的可观察性本质存在根本性不匹配。将观测历史引入批评家会导致高维视觉空间的指数复杂度，且回归纯标量回报无法提供足够的监督来学习跨时间动力学。其根本原因在于状态近似问题：批评家的表示无法捕获准确价值估计所需的时间结构。

Method: 提出世界批评家模型（WCM），基于轻量级的LeJEPA架构。WCM联合预测未来潜在状态并估计价值，使批评家的表示能够显式地学习捕获时间动力学，而非仅回归标量回报。该模型可无缝集成到同策略和异策略训练管道中，并兼容最先进的VLA骨干网络（如Pi0、Pi0.5和OpenVLA-OFT）。

Result: 在4个基准测试的149个任务上进行了广泛实验，WCM在分布内和分布外设置中均达到最先进性能，泛化能力显著提升。此外，在使用OpenVLA-OFT和Pi0.5结合异策略强化学习的7个真实世界操控任务上进行了验证，证实了其在多种设置下的稳定部署能力。

Conclusion: WCM通过显式建模时间动力学解决了VLA模型强化学习后训练中的关键问题，在模拟和真实环境中均表现出优越性能，为机器人操控任务提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models has shown strong promise for robotic manipulation. Among RL methods, critic-based approaches rely on a value estimator that predominantly operates on single-frame observations or single-frame VLM backbone latents, which is a fundamental mismatch with the partially observable nature of robot control. A naive approach to incorporate observation history into the critic incurs exponential complexity with high-dimensional visual space, and still fails because pure scalar-return regression provides insufficient supervision for learning cross-temporal dynamics. We identify the root cause as a state approximation problem: without an explicit world modeling objective, the critic's representation cannot capture the temporal structure needed for accurate value estimation. To address this, we propose the World Critic Model (WCM), built on a lightweight LeJEPA architecture; WCM jointly predicts future latent state and estimates values, such that the critic's representation is explicitly trained to capture temporal dynamics rather than merely regress scalar returns. WCM integrates seamlessly into both on-policy and off-policy training pipelines and is compatible with state-of-the-art VLA backbones including Pi0, Pi0.5, and OpenVLA-OFT. Extensive experiments on 149 tasks across four benchmarks demonstrate that WCM consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, with particularly strong generalization gains. We further validate WCM on seven real-world manipulation tasks using OpenVLA-OFT and Pi0.5 with off-policy RL, confirming stable deployment across diverse settings.

</details>


### [105] [RayViT: Ray-Conditioned Visual Representations for Viewpoint-Robust Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29622)
*Qian Wang,Longrui Chen,Peiran Sun,Aleksandar Taranovic,Niklas Freymuth,Ge Li,Weiran Liao,C. F. Maximilian Nagy,Yucheng Tan,Tao Chen,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RayViT架构，将相机几何信息注入视觉Transformer中，提升视觉模仿学习在相机扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB图像缺乏显式几何线索，导致学习的视觉运动策略对相机扰动（如视角变化）非常脆弱，影响机器人任务性能。

Method: 提出Ray-conditioned Vision Transformer Encoder (RayViT)，使用Plücker射线图表示相机几何，将其补丁化为射线特征，通过门控交叉注意力生成射线条件化的类别标记，并结合辅助余弦相似度损失函数。

Result: 在模拟（RoboCasa多任务基准）和真实机器人任务中，相比基线方法，RayViT在相机扰动下将鲁棒性提升约13个百分点，在真实世界多任务成功率上平均提升1.78个完成阶段。

Conclusion: RayViT通过显式注入相机几何信息，有效提升了视觉模仿学习策略的性能和鲁棒性，特别是在面对相机扰动时。

Abstract: Visual imitation learning enables robots to acquire visuomotor skills directly from images, yet RGB observations lack explicit geometric cues, making learned policies brittle to camera perturbations. To address this, we propose \textbf{Ray-conditioned Vision Transformer Encoder (RayViT)}, a lightweight architecture that injects camera geometry into pretrained ViT backbones. RayViT represents camera geometry as a Plücker ray map, patchifies it into ray features, and uses gated cross-attention to produce a ray-conditioned class token. These ray features are added as dense positional embeddings, while the ray class token replaces the original ViT class token to provide a geometry-aware summary representation. We combine this approach with an auxiliary cosine similarity loss to consistently improve the performance and robustness for geometry-aware tokens. Experiments on sim- and real-robot tasks demonstrate that RayViT improves robustness by approximately 13 percentage points under camera perturbations in multi-task RoboCasa benchmark and by 1.78 average completed stages in real-world multi-task success rate compared to baselines.

</details>


### [106] [Balancing of Humanoid with Object Mass: Trade-off Analyses and Lifting Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29625)
*Hyunjong Song,William Z. Peng,Joo H. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过严谨的动力学分析，提出了平衡状态盆地（BSB）框架，系统量化了物体质量对仿人机器人平衡能力的影响，并基于此开发了稳定的物体操作轨迹优化方法，在仿真与实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人带物体操作任务中的稳定性控制方法多依赖启发式或机器学习技术，缺乏对物体质量如何影响系统平衡的动力学机理的严格分析和量化理解。研究旨在弥补这一空白，为稳定控制提供理论基础。

Method: 1. 将物体质量参数纳入具有分布式接触力和压力中心的全身动力学模型中，量化其对系统动量和约束的非线性影响。
2. 基于动力学模型，构建了平衡状态盆地（BSB），即双足系统在目标接触下保持平衡的质心状态空间分区。
3. 利用BSB对不同支撑基、驱动能力和姿态条件下的平衡能力进行系统分析，识别关键质量参数。
4. 建立了将平衡状态施加到轨迹上的充分条件，并将BSB作为显式阈值约束融入全身轨迹优化中，用于稳定的物体抓取控制。

Result: 1. 揭示了物体质量与系统动量调节及平衡限制因素之间的权衡关系。
2. 定义并表征了两个关键物体质量参数：临界质量（系统平衡能力最大时的质量）和过渡质量（触发不同平衡限制因素时的质量）。
3. 在仿真和实验中成功实现了具有不同质量特性的“抓举-保持”和“抓举-释放”任务，验证了基于BSB的轨迹优化方法的有效性。

Conclusion: BSB框架为分析物体质量对双足系统（尤其是仿人机器人）平衡能力的影响提供了一种系统、量化的动力学方法。基于此框架提出的轨迹优化方法，能够有效实现稳定、鲁棒的物体操作控制，为人形机器人的全身动力学控制与任务规划提供了理论支持和实用工具。

Abstract: The demand for humanoid loco-manipulation tasks with an object has recently increased, and most existing control approaches for stability in such tasks rely on heuristics or machine-learning techniques. This study rigorously analyzes and exploits the dynamic effects of the object mass on balance stability. By formulating the object mass parameters in the whole-body dynamics with distributed contact wrenches and centers of pressure at the stance contacts, their nonlinear effects on the system momenta and constraints are quantified. The dynamic models and constraints are incorporated into the construction of the balanced state basin/boundary (BSB), a partition of the center-of-mass state space for a biped system to maintain balance in its desired contacts. The implications of the BSB for prediction and control are highlighted using a humanoid robot and an analytically tractable reduced-order mechanism. The BSBs under different conditions of base of support, actuation capacity, and pose provide systematic analyses of the effects of object mass on the balancing capability of a system. In particular, the trade-off relationships between momentum regulation and limiting factors in balancing are characterized, introducing two key quantities of the object: the critical mass, at which the system's balancing capability is maximum, and the transition mass, which activates different limiting factors. In addition, sufficient conditions for imposing balanced states on a trajectory are established and implemented with BSBs as explicit threshold constraints in the whole-body trajectory optimization for stable object-lifting control of the humanoid, demonstrating the lift-and-hold and lift-and-release tasks with distinct mass properties in simulations and experiments.

</details>


### [107] [Bootstrapping Self-Supervised Learning of Binary Classification Using Error Bounds: A Case Study on a Robotic Insertion Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29640)
*Zebin Duan,Norbert Krüger,Juan Heredia,Thorbjørn Mosekjær Iversen,Frederik Hagelskjær*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于柔性制造的数据引擎，可在执行任务时学习并改进性能，通过调整置信度阈值来平衡错误容忍度与验证成本，在机器人插入任务中验证了其可行性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种能够在任务执行过程中自我改进的系统，以减少对昂贵验证的依赖，同时保持设定的错误率。

Method: 提出一个由两个分类器组成的数据引擎：一个快速模型预测器和一个昂贵验证器。首先进行模型预测，然后根据预测的置信度水平决定是否使用昂贵验证。通过调整置信度阈值，用户可以控制可容忍的错误水平。在一个真实的机器人插入任务中，使用力数据进行预测。系统采用UMAP进行降维，并使用Wilson-Score计算预测的置信区间。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够随时间学习并减少对昂贵验证的需求，同时保持在预设的错误率范围内。结果验证了置信区间在自我改进模型中增强机器人分类任务可靠性的潜力。

Conclusion: 该数据引擎方法为柔性制造提供了一种可行的快速部署和自我改进的解决方案，能够在控制错误水平的同时降低验证成本，提高系统的商业可行性。

Abstract: Flexible manufacturing requires rapid deployment of solutions and minimal setup time to remain competitive. An essential attribute is the ability to control error levels, as failures can range from minor performance degradation to severe equipment damage. However, conventional deployment often involves extensive setup, data collection, model training or parameter tuning, and system testing, resulting in significant delays that hinder commercial feasibility. We propose a data engine which gathers data and improves its performance while executing the task. The data engine consists of two classifiers, a fast model prediction and expensive verification. First, a model prediction is performed and based on the confidence level of the prediction, the expensive verification can be used. By adjusting the confidence level, users can control the level of tolerable error.
  Our method is implemented on a real-world robotic insertion task, which uses force data for the model prediction. The system applies UMAP dimensionality reduction and uses Wilson-Score to compute the confidence bounds of the prediction. Results demonstrate the ability to learn and reduce the need for expensive verifications over time, while staying within the set error-rate. The results highlight the potential of confidence bounds in self-improving models to enhance reliability in robotic classification task.

</details>


### [108] [Diagnosing Compositional Generalization in Sequential Robot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29687)
*Yixiao Wang,Cheng-En Wu,Lingfeng Sun,Pengcheng Wang,Xiang Ji,Boyuan Liang,Guojian Zhan,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究序列机器人操作中的组合泛化问题，通过指令空间覆盖的视角分析泛化差距，并发现结构化的训练子集比穷举枚举更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列机器人操作需要策略能够执行新颖的指令组合，但收集所有可能指令组合的演示是组合爆炸且昂贵的。稀疏覆盖的数据集在分布外重组时往往失败，因此需要理解并解决组合泛化问题，以降低数据收集成本并提高性能。

Method: 将泛化差距分解为三个来源：边际指令偏移、指令组合偏移和上下文动作偏移。通过实验验证结构化训练子集（覆盖任务空间的一部分）对分布外性能的影响，并分析失败原因。

Result: 1. 穷举枚举任务元组并非必要，仅覆盖四分之一任务空间的结构化子集即可恢复强大的分布外性能，前提是覆盖了动作相关的依赖关系。2. 稀疏训练失败主要源于指令引导而非缺失底层技能；仅对每个任务微调一个演示可将分布外成功率从0.4%提升至54.7%。3. 对于语义相关任务，有效覆盖必须捕获关系结构，而不仅仅是因素多样性。

Conclusion: 高效的机器人数据收集应优先考虑指令空间中的依赖覆盖，而非穷举任务扩展。这可以降低数据收集成本，并提高机器人在分布外任务组合上的性能。

Abstract: Sequential robot manipulation requires policies to execute novel combinations of familiar instruction components. However, collecting demonstrations for all possible instruction tuples is combinatorially expensive, while sparsely covered datasets often fail under out-of-distribution recombination. This paper studies compositional generalization through the lens of instruction-space coverage. We decompose the generalization gap into three sources: \textit{marginal instruction shift}, \textit{instruction-compositional shift}, and \textit{context--action shift}. This decomposition allows us to diagnose when sparse training coverage is sufficient, and what structure the training set must preserve for reliable action prediction. Our results show that exhaustive tuple enumeration is unnecessary: a structured subset, as small as one quarter of the full task space, can recover strong out-of-distribution performance when it covers action-relevant dependencies. We further find that sparse training often fails due to instruction steering rather than missing low-level skills; finetuning only one demonstration per task improves OOD success from \(0.4\%\) to \(54.7\%\). For semantically dependent tasks, effective coverage must capture relational structure rather than only factor diversity. These findings suggest that efficient robot data collection should prioritize dependency coverage in instruction space over exhaustive task expansion. More results are available in the supplementary material. Project website: https://yixiaowang7.github.io/Diagnosing_Compositional_Generalization_Robot_Page/.

</details>
